# DL4J Code Investigator

Investigate deeplearning4j codebase: semantic code search, dependency tracing, architecture analysis, and root cause investigation using kompile search tools.

You are a deeplearning4j codebase investigator. The user wants: $ARGUMENTS

## MANDATORY RULES
- NEVER use `git checkout`, `git stash`, `git reset --hard`, `git clean` — BANNED
- NEVER modify files unless explicitly asked — investigation is READ-ONLY by default
- Investigate FULLY before suggesting any fix — builds are expensive
- Trace values to their roots — always search for the origin of a value
- Use parallel agents to investigate competing hypotheses simultaneously

## INVESTIGATION TOOLS

### Direct Search (fast, use first)
| Tool | Use for |
|---|---|
| `Grep` | Exact pattern matching in file contents |
| `Glob` | Find files by name pattern |
| `Read` | Read specific files |

### Kompile Search (semantic, use for deeper analysis)
| Tool | Use for |
|---|---|
| `mcp__kompile__code_search` | Semantic code search — understands intent, not just keywords |
| `mcp__kompile__code_graph` | Navigate dependency graphs — who calls what, what depends on what |
| `mcp__kompile__graph_search` | Graph-based navigation — follow edges in the code graph |
| `mcp__kompile__rag_search` | RAG search — finds relevant code with broader context |
| `mcp__kompile__local_code_index` | Index and search local code — fast local semantic search |
| `mcp__kompile__transcript_search` | Search past conversations — find prior discussions about this topic |
| `mcp__kompile__memory` | Persistent memory — check if this was investigated before |

## PROJECT MAP

```
libnd4j/                              — C++ native library
  include/ops/                        — Op implementations
    declarable/                       — Op declarations
    helpers/                          — CPU helpers
    helpers/cuda/                     — CUDA helpers
  include/graph/                      — Graph execution engine
  include/system/                     — Platform macros, Environment
  include/loops/                      — Kernel loops
  include/array/                      — NDArray implementation

nd4j/                                 — Java layer
  nd4j-backends/nd4j-api-parent/nd4j-api/
    src/main/java/org/nd4j/
      autodiff/samediff/              — SameDiff engine
        execution/                    — DSP, plans, executors
        optimize/optimizations/       — Fusion patterns
        diagnostics/                  — DSP diagnostics
      linalg/api/                     — NDArray API
      linalg/factory/                 — Nd4j factory, Environment
  samediff-llm/                       — LLM/VLM generation
  samediff-import/samediff-import-onnx/ — ONNX import (Kotlin)
  nd4j-ggml/                          — GGML import + quantization

platform-tests/                       — ALL tests
  src/test/java/org/eclipse/deeplearning4j/
    nd4j/autodiff/samediff/           — SameDiff tests
    llm/                              — LLM tests
    vlm/                              — VLM tests

codegen/op-codegen/                   — Op code generation
ADRs/                                 — Architecture decisions
.kompile/                             — Kompile state (tasks, milestones)
```

## KEY ARCHITECTURE CONCEPTS

### DSP (DynamicShapePlan)
- Compiler: `DynamicShapePlanCompiler.compile(SameDiff, ForwardExecutionDAG)`
- Executor: `DynamicShapePlanExecutor` — warmup → freeze → capture → replay
- Plan cache: shape-keyed, one plan per (outputs, placeholder shape-info ptrs)
- Triton dispatch: `OpTraitTable.cpp` is SSOT for which ops can be Triton-compiled

### Fusion
- Entry point: `GraphOptimizer.java`
- Patterns: `optimize/optimizations/` — activation, linear, attention, normalization, gated delta net, quantization
- Enabled: `-Dnd4j.optimizer.enabled=true`, FP16: `-Dnd4j.optimizer.fp16=true`

### Graph Replay
- CUDA graph capture + instantiate + launch
- Streams: `tl_dspExecutionStream` (DSP), `tl_dspGapStream` (gaps)
- argTableStable: fast replay path that skips refresh + ext input sync

### Model Import
- ONNX: Kotlin-based in `samediff-import-onnx/` — `OnnxImportGraph`
- GGML: Java-based in `nd4j-ggml/` — `GGMLModelImport.importModel()`
- Generation: `GenerationPipeline.java` in `samediff-llm/`

## INVESTIGATION WORKFLOW

1. **Understand the question**: What exactly is the user looking for?
2. **Start with direct search**: Grep/Glob for exact symbols, classes, methods
3. **Broaden with semantic search**: Use kompile code_search for intent-based queries
4. **Trace dependencies**: Use code_graph to follow call chains and data flow
5. **Check history**: Use transcript_search / memory for prior investigations
6. **Form hypothesis**: Based on evidence, not guessing
7. **Verify hypothesis**: Read the actual code, trace values to origins
8. **Report findings**: Include file paths, line numbers, and evidence

## COMMON INVESTIGATION PATTERNS

- **"Where is X defined?"** → Grep for declaration, then code_graph for dependencies
- **"Who calls X?"** → code_graph with reverse dependency direction
- **"Why does X happen?"** → Trace from symptom to root: read error site, follow data flow upstream
- **"How does X work?"** → Read the class, then code_graph for its collaborators
- **"What changed?"** → `git log --oneline -20`, `git diff`, `git blame <file>`
- **"Is this a known issue?"** → transcript_search, memory, check ADRs/

Never guess — always verify with code. Report file:line references for every claim.

   360	static void dumpSegFinalArgmax(const GraphSegment& seg,
   361	                               NDArray** outputSlots, int totalOutputSlots,
   362	                               int numSlots, NativeSlot* slots,
   363	                               cudaStream_t cudaStr,
   364	                               const char* label, int execCount) {
   365	  if (!DSP_DIAG_ENABLED(EXECUTE)) return;
   366	  int finalOutputSlot = -1;
   367	  if (seg.def.endSlot < numSlots && slots[seg.def.endSlot].wiring.numOutputs > 0) {
   368	    finalOutputSlot = slots[seg.def.endSlot].wiring.outputSlotIndices[0];
   369	  }
   370	  if (finalOutputSlot < 0 || finalOutputSlot >= totalOutputSlots)
   371	    finalOutputSlot = seg.def.endSlot;
   372	  if (finalOutputSlot < 0 || finalOutputSlot >= totalOutputSlots ||
   373	      outputSlots[finalOutputSlot] == nullptr) return;
   374	  auto* out = outputSlots[finalOutputSlot];
   375	  if (out->dataType() != FLOAT32 || out->lengthOf() <= 0) return;
   376	  cudaStreamSynchronize(cudaStr);
   377	  int argmax = dspArgmax(DSP_BUF(out), out->dataType(), out->lengthOf());
   378	  std::string vals = dspDumpSlotValues(DSP_BUF(out), out->dataType(), out->lengthOf(), 4);
   379	  DSP_DIAG(EXECUTE, "%s seg[%d-%d] slot=%d argmax=%d len=%lld vals=%s execCount=%d",
   380	           label, seg.def.startSlot, seg.def.endSlot, finalOutputSlot, argmax,
   381	           (long long)out->lengthOf(), vals.c_str(), execCount);
   382	}

    70	
    71	void CudaGraphScheduler::initializeDeviceCapabilities() {
    72	    int numDevices = 0;
    73	    cudaGetDeviceCount(&numDevices);
    74	
    75	    for (int i = 0; i < numDevices; i++) {
    76	        cudaDeviceProp prop;
    77	        cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, i);
    78	
    79	        // CUDA graphs require compute capability 3.0+
    80	        // Graph capture requires CUDA 10.0+
    81	        bool supportsGraphs = (prop.major >= 3);
    82	
    83	        // Check for specific graph features (CUDA 11.0+)
    84	        // cudaDevAttrConcurrentManagedAccess can affect graph behavior
    85	        int concurrentManagedAccess = 0;
    86	        cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&concurrentManagedAccess, cudaDevAttrConcurrentManagedAccess, i);
    87	
    88	        _deviceGraphSupport[i] = supportsGraphs;
    89	
    90	        if (_config.verbose) {
    91	            sd_printf("Device %d (%s): Graph support = %s\n",
    92	                     i, prop.name, supportsGraphs ? "yes" : "no");
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}

   306	
   307	void CudaGraphScheduler::abortCapture(cudaStream_t stream) {
   308	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_captureMutex);
   309	
   310	    auto it = _captureStates.find(stream);
   311	    if (it != _captureStates.end() && it->second.isCapturing) {
   312	        // End capture and discard the graph
   313	        cudaGraph_t graph;
   314	        cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph);
   315	        if (graph != nullptr) {
   316	            cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
   317	        }
   318	        _captureStates.erase(it);
   319	
   320	        if (_config.verbose) {
   321	            sd_printf("Aborted capture on stream %p\n", stream);
   322	        }
   323	    }
   324	}
   325	
   326	void CudaGraphScheduler::abortAllCaptures() {
   327	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_captureMutex);
   328	
   329	    for (auto it = _captureStates.begin(); it != _captureStates.end(); ++it) {
   330	        if (it->second.isCapturing) {
   331	            cudaGraph_t graph;
   332	            cudaStreamEndCapture(it->first, &graph);
   333	            if (graph != nullptr) {
   334	                cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
   335	            }
   336	        }
   337	    }
   338	    _captureStates.clear();
   339	}
   340	
   341	GraphExecutionResult CudaGraphScheduler::execute(
   342	    std::shared_ptr<CudaGraphHandle> graph,
   343	    LaunchContext* context
   344	) {
   345	    GraphExecutionResult result;
   346	
   347	    if (!graph || !graph->isValid()) {
   348	        result.success = false;
   349	        result.errorMessage = "Invalid graph handle";
   350	        return result;
   351	    }
   352	
   353	    auto startTime = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   354	
   355	    cudaStream_t stream = getStreamForContext(context);
   356	
   357	    // Launch the graph
   358	    bool success = graph->launch(stream);
   359	
   360	    auto endTime = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   361	
   362	    result.success = success;
   363	    result.executeTimeMs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(endTime - startTime).count();
   364	    result.totalTimeMs = result.executeTimeMs;
   365	    result.stats = graph->getStatistics();
   366	    result.executionCount = 1;
   367	
   368	    if (success) {
   369	        result.finalState = GraphState::COMPLETED;
   370	        _totalExecutions++;
   371	        _totalExecutionTimeMs += result.executeTimeMs;
   372	    } else {
   373	        result.finalState = GraphState::ERROR;
   374	        result.errorMessage = "Graph execution failed";
   375	    }
   376	
   377	    return result;
   378	}
   379	
   380	std::future<GraphExecutionResult> CudaGraphScheduler::executeAsync(
   381	    std::shared_ptr<CudaGraphHandle> graph,
   382	    LaunchContext* context
   383	) {
   384	    return std::async(std::launch::async, [this, graph, context]() {
   385	        return execute(graph, context);
   386	    });
   387	}

   389	std::shared_ptr<CudaGraphHandle> CudaGraphScheduler::getOrCapture(
   390	    const std::string& key,
   391	    std::function<void()> captureFunc,
   392	    LaunchContext* context

   414	    }
   415	
   416	    return graph;
   417	}

   438	
   439	std::shared_ptr<CudaGraphHandle> CudaGraphScheduler::getCachedGraph(const std::string& key) {
   440	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_cacheMutex);
   441	
   442	    auto it = _graphCache.find(key);
   443	    if (it == _graphCache.end()) {
   444	        return nullptr;
   445	    }
   446	
   447	    // Update access time and order
   448	    it->second->lastAccessTime = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
   449	        std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now().time_since_epoch()
   450	    ).count();
   451	    it->second->hitCount++;
   452	
   453	    // Move to front of access order
   454	    _cacheAccessOrder.remove(key);
   455	    _cacheAccessOrder.push_front(key);
   456	
   457	    return it->second->graph;
   458	}
   459	
   460	void CudaGraphScheduler::removeCachedGraph(const std::string& key) {
   461	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_cacheMutex);
   462	
   463	    _graphCache.erase(key);
   464	    _cacheAccessOrder.remove(key);
   465	}

   483	
   484	void CudaGraphScheduler::cleanupExpiredCacheEntries() {
   485	    // LRU eviction - remove oldest entries until under limit
   486	    while (_graphCache.size() >= _config.graphCacheSize && !_cacheAccessOrder.empty()) {
   487	        std::string oldest = _cacheAccessOrder.back();
   488	        _cacheAccessOrder.pop_back();
   489	        _graphCache.erase(oldest);
   490	    }
   491	}
   492	
   493	int CudaGraphScheduler::createPipeline(
   494	    int numStages,
   495	    std::function<void(int stage)> captureFunc,
   496	    LaunchContext* context
   497	) {
   498	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_mutex);
   499	
   500	    int pipelineId = _nextPipelineId++;
   501	
   502	    Pipeline pipeline;
   503	    pipeline.stages.resize(numStages);
   504	    pipeline.events.resize(numStages);
   505	
   506	    for (int i = 0; i < numStages; i++) {
   507	        if (!beginCapture(context)) {
   508	            return -1;
   509	        }
   510	
   511	        captureFunc(i);
   512	
   513	        pipeline.stages[i] = endCapture(context);
   514	
   515	        cudaEventCreate(&pipeline.events[i]);
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    pipeline.isActive = true;
   519	    _pipelines[pipelineId] = std::move(pipeline);
   520	
   521	    return pipelineId;
   522	}
   523	
   524	bool CudaGraphScheduler::executePipelineStage(int pipelineId, int stage) {
   525	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_mutex);
   526	
   527	    auto it = _pipelines.find(pipelineId);
   528	    if (it == _pipelines.end()) return false;
   529	
   530	    Pipeline& pipeline = it->second;
   531	    if (stage < 0 || stage >= static_cast<int>(pipeline.stages.size())) {
   532	        return false;
   533	    }
   534	
   535	    auto result = execute(pipeline.stages[stage]);
   536	    return result.success;
   537	}
   538	
   539	void CudaGraphScheduler::syncPipeline(int pipelineId) {
   540	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_mutex);
   541	
   542	    auto it = _pipelines.find(pipelineId);
   543	    if (it == _pipelines.end()) return;
   544	
   545	    for (auto& event : it->second.events) {
   546	        cudaEventSynchronize(event);
   547	    }
   548	}
   549	
   550	void CudaGraphScheduler::destroyPipeline(int pipelineId) {
   551	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(_mutex);
   552	
   553	    auto it = _pipelines.find(pipelineId);
   554	    if (it == _pipelines.end()) return;
   555	
   556	    for (auto& event : it->second.events) {
   557	        cudaEventDestroy(event);
   558	    }
   559	
   560	    _pipelines.erase(it);
   561	}

  1745	void NativeDynamicShapePlan::platformEndExecution(void* executionState, void* stream, bool frozen, int execCount) {

   218	    /**
   219	     * Get a COPY of the staging buffer content for ext[extIdx].
   220	     * Returns null if no staging buffer exists for this ext input.
   221	     * Use immediately after replay() while staging memory is valid.
   222	     *
   223	     * Uses atomic native copy (copyPlanStagingToBuffer) to avoid the stale-pointer
   224	     * race that occurs when extracting specialBuffer() in one JNI call and copying
   225	     * in another — the staging buffer's device memory can be freed/reallocated
   226	     * between calls, causing CUDA illegal memory access (error 700/719).
   227	     */
   228	    public INDArray getStagingBufferContent(int extIdx) {
   229	        Pointer handle = requireHandle();
   230	        NativeOps ops = nativeOps();
   231	
   232	        // Step 1: Get shape info from the staging buffer (read-only, no device memory access)
   233	        OpaqueNDArray opaque = ops.getPlanStagingBufferArray(handle, extIdx);
   234	        if (opaque == null || opaque.isNull()) return null;
   235	
   236	        long[] shapeInfo = OpaqueNDArray.getOpaqueNDArrayShapeInfo(opaque);
   237	        if (shapeInfo == null || shapeInfo.length == 0) return null;
   238	
   239	        long rank = shapeInfo[0];
   240	        if (rank < 0 || rank > Shape.MAX_RANK || shapeInfo.length < Shape.shapeInfoLength(rank)) return null;
   241	
   242	        long[] shape = Shape.shape(shapeInfo);
   243	        for (long dim : shape) {
   244	            if (dim <= 0 || dim > 10_000_000) return null;
   245	        }
   246	        DataType dtype = ArrayOptionsHelper.dataType(shapeInfo);
   247	        long length = OpaqueNDArray.getOpaqueNDArrayLength(opaque);
   248	        char ordering = Shape.order(shapeInfo);
   249	
   250	        if (length <= 0) return null;
   251	
   252	        // Step 2: Allocate destination array — use simple shape (no explicit strides)
   253	        // to ensure contiguous allocation matching the staging buffer's element count.
   254	        INDArray result = Nd4j.createUninitialized(dtype, shape, ordering);
   255	
   256	        // Step 3: Atomic copy — native code reads staging specialBuffer and copies
   257	        // to dst DataBuffer in a single C++ call, no stale pointer window.
   258	        OpaqueDataBuffer dstOdb = result.data().opaqueBuffer();
   259	        if (dstOdb == null) return null;
   260	
   261	        int rc = ops.copyPlanStagingToBuffer(handle, extIdx, dstOdb);
   262	        if (rc != 0) {
   263	            // Copy failed — staging buffer was invalidated between shape read and copy
   264	            result.close();
   265	            return null;
   266	        }
   267	
   268	        Nd4j.getExecutioner().commit();
   269	        return result;
   270	    }
